Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bayesian operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 938. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 105 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the bayesian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^938 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 105-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
